      "answerQuery": "PREFIX agents: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology#>\nPREFIX dcat: <http://www.w3.org/ns/dcat#>\nPREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>\nPREFIX eli: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/ontology#>\nPREFIX eli-dl: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/eli-draft-legislation-ontology#>\nPREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>\nPREFIX members: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology/members#>\nPREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>\nPREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>\nPREFIX skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#>\nPREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>\nSELECT DISTINCT ?member ?memberName ?membership ?membershipRange ?membershipStart ?membershipEnd ?episode ?party ?partyLabel ?partyRange ?partyStart ?partyEnd ?affiliation ?affiliationLabel ?term WHERE {\nGRAPH ?memberGraph { ?member a agents:Member . ?membership a members:DailMembership . ?episode a members:PartyMembership . ?member members:hasMembersMembership ?membership . ?membership members:inHouseTerm ?term . ?member members:hasMembersMembership ?episode . ?episode members:inOireachtasMembership ?membership . ?episode members:isPartyMembershipOf ?party . OPTIONAL { ?member foaf:name ?memberName . } OPTIONAL { ?membership members:hasMembershipDateRange ?membershipRange .\n      ?membershipRange a members:DateRange .\n      OPTIONAL { ?membershipRange members:StartDate ?membershipStart . }\n      OPTIONAL { ?membershipRange members:EndDate ?membershipEnd . } } OPTIONAL { ?episode members:hasMembershipDateRange ?partyRange .\n      ?partyRange a members:DateRange .\n      OPTIONAL { ?partyRange members:StartDate ?partyStart . }\n      OPTIONAL { ?partyRange members:EndDate ?partyEnd . } } } FILTER(STRSTARTS(STR(?memberGraph), \"https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/member/\"))\nGRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/houses> { ?term a agents:DailTerm . VALUES ?term { <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ie/oireachtas/house/dail/34> } }\nGRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/parties> { ?party a members:ParliamentaryParty . VALUES ?party { <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ie/oireachtas/party/dail/34/Labour_Party> } ?party members:activeDuringTerm ?term . OPTIONAL { ?party skos:prefLabel ?partyLabel . FILTER(LANG(?partyLabel) = \"en\") } }\nOPTIONAL { GRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/political-affiliations/identities> { ?affiliation a members:PoliticalAffiliationIdentity . ?party members:hasPoliticalAffiliationIdentity ?affiliation . OPTIONAL { ?affiliation skos:prefLabel ?affiliationLabel . FILTER(LANG(?affiliationLabel) = \"en\") } } }\n} ORDER BY ?affiliationLabel ?memberName ?partyStart ?membershipStart ?episode ?membership LIMIT 100",

        "sparql": "PREFIX agents: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology#>\nPREFIX dcat: <http://www.w3.org/ns/dcat#>\nPREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>\nPREFIX eli: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/ontology#>\nPREFIX eli-dl: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/eli-draft-legislation-ontology#>\nPREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>\nPREFIX members: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology/members#>\nPREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>\nPREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>\nPREFIX skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#>\nPREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>\nSELECT ?term ?start ?end WHERE {\n  GRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/houses> {\n    ?term a agents:DailTerm ; dct:temporal ?period .\n    ?period dcat:startDate ?start .\n    OPTIONAL { ?period dcat:endDate ?end . }\n  }\n} LIMIT 100",

        "sparql": "PREFIX agents: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology#>\nPREFIX dcat: <http://www.w3.org/ns/dcat#>\nPREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>\nPREFIX eli: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/ontology#>\nPREFIX eli-dl: <http://data.europa.eu/eli/eli-draft-legislation-ontology#>\nPREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>\nPREFIX members: <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ontology/members#>\nPREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>\nPREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>\nPREFIX skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#>\nPREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>\nSELECT DISTINCT ?member ?memberName ?membership ?membershipRange ?membershipStart ?membershipEnd ?episode ?party ?partyLabel ?partyRange ?partyStart ?partyEnd ?affiliation ?affiliationLabel ?term WHERE {\nGRAPH ?memberGraph { ?member a agents:Member . ?membership a members:DailMembership . ?episode a members:PartyMembership . ?member members:hasMembersMembership ?membership . ?membership members:inHouseTerm ?term . ?member members:hasMembersMembership ?episode . ?episode members:inOireachtasMembership ?membership . ?episode members:isPartyMembershipOf ?party . OPTIONAL { ?member foaf:name ?memberName . } OPTIONAL { ?membership members:hasMembershipDateRange ?membershipRange .\n      ?membershipRange a members:DateRange .\n      OPTIONAL { ?membershipRange members:StartDate ?membershipStart . }\n      OPTIONAL { ?membershipRange members:EndDate ?membershipEnd . } } OPTIONAL { ?episode members:hasMembershipDateRange ?partyRange .\n      ?partyRange a members:DateRange .\n      OPTIONAL { ?partyRange members:StartDate ?partyStart . }\n      OPTIONAL { ?partyRange members:EndDate ?partyEnd . } } } FILTER(STRSTARTS(STR(?memberGraph), \"https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/member/\"))\nGRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/houses> { ?term a agents:DailTerm . VALUES ?term { <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ie/oireachtas/house/dail/34> } }\nGRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/parties> { ?party a members:ParliamentaryParty . VALUES ?party { <https://data.oireachtas.ie/ie/oireachtas/party/dail/34/Fine_Gael> } ?party members:activeDuringTerm ?term . OPTIONAL { ?party skos:prefLabel ?partyLabel . FILTER(LANG(?partyLabel) = \"en\") } }\nOPTIONAL { GRAPH <https://data.oireachtas.ie/graph/political-affiliations/identities> { ?affiliation a members:PoliticalAffiliationIdentity . ?party members:hasPoliticalAffiliationIdentity ?affiliation . OPTIONAL { ?affiliation skos:prefLabel ?affiliationLabel . FILTER(LANG(?affiliationLabel) = \"en\") } } }\n} ORDER BY ?affiliationLabel ?memberName ?partyStart ?membershipStart ?episode ?membership LIMIT 100",
